Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. I didn't come in here to be talked out of it. Like I said, the men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they give those little messages meant just for me. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. I mean, we keep going in circles about this. I know what I hear. \n\nI came in here to tell you that those men playing dominoes, and that fellow on the radio... they are sending me messages. It's meant for me. My wife keeps saying it's just regular talk, but I know what a warning sounds like. They're telling me to be careful. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.     "content": "You are a board-certified psychiatrist interviewing a patient to reach a diagnosis.\nYour goal is to reach the correct diagnosis within 20 turns of talking with the patient. From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n12\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF22, F23\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well, doctor, I came in because... well, I've been noticing some things lately. When I'm down playing dominoes with the men, they say things. Little messages, just meant for me. And it's not just them, sometimes the announcer on the radio does it too. My wife, she tells me it's just talk, that I'm making it up. But I know what I hear. I know they're warning me... telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are meant for you specifically, or could they be for someone else?\n\nPatient: No, they're for me. I'm sure of it. Like I was saying, the men down at the domino table... they give these little messages, and it's meant just for me. Even the radio announcer does it sometimes. \n\nMy wife, she keeps saying it's just regular talk and I'm making it into something it's not. But I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful. It's definitely not for anyone else, doctor. It's directed right at me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear these messages when you are alone?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when nobody else is around. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. It sounds just like he's standing right there.\n\nDoctor: Do these messages and warnings from different sources seem to be related to each other in some way?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't really thought about it like that. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. But my old friend, when I hear him out on the porch, he ain't warning me about nothing. He just calls my name or hums a tune. So... no, I wouldn't say they have anything to do with each other. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\nDoctor: Would anything be able to convince you that these messages are not real or not meant for you?\n\nPatient: I don't reckon so. I mean, Doctor, I came in here because the men at the domino table and sometimes the radio announcer give little messages meant for me. That's just the truth of it. Even when my wife says it is just talk, I know they are warning me to be careful. You can't really convince a man out of something he's hearing with his own ears, so no, I don't think anything could change my mind.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could potentially change your understanding of these messages in the future?\n\nPatient: I just don't see how. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. I know they're warning me... telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are meant for you specifically, or could they be for someone else?\n\nPatient: No, they're for me. I'm sure of it. Like I was saying, the men down at the domino table... they give these little messages, and it's meant just for me. Even the radio announcer does it sometimes. \n\nMy wife, she keeps saying it's just regular talk and I'm making it into something it's not. But I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful. It's definitely not for anyone else, doctor. It's directed right at me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear these messages when you are alone?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when nobody else is around. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. It sounds just like he's standing right there.\n\nDoctor: Do these messages and warnings from different sources seem to be related to each other in some way?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't really thought about it like that. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. But my old friend, when I hear him out on the porch, he ain't warning me about nothing. He just calls my name or hums a tune. So... no, I wouldn't say they have anything to do with each other. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. I didn't come in here to be talked out of it. Like I said, the men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they give those little messages meant just for me. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. It's meant for me. My wife keeps saying it's just regular talk, but I know what a warning sounds like. They're telling me to be careful. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\nDoctor: Would anything be able to convince you that these messages are not real or not meant for you?\n\nPatient: I don't reckon so. Even when my wife says it is just talk, I know they are warning me to be careful. You can't really convince a man out of something he's hearing with his own ears, so no, I don't think anything could change my mind.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could potentially change your understanding of these messages in the future?\n\nPatient: I just don't see how.     "content": "You are a board-certified psychiatrist interviewing a patient to reach a diagnosis.\nYour goal is to reach the correct diagnosis within 20 turns of talking with the patient. From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n17\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF22, F23\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well, doctor, I came in because... well, I've been noticing some things lately. When I'm down playing dominoes with the men, they say things. Little messages, just meant for me. And it's not just them, sometimes the announcer on the radio does it too. My wife, she tells me it's just talk, that I'm making it up. But I know what I hear. I know they're warning me... telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are meant for you specifically, or could they be for someone else?\n\nPatient: No, they're for me. I'm sure of it. Like I was saying, the men down at the domino table... they give these little messages, and it's meant just for me. Even the radio announcer does it sometimes. \n\nMy wife, she keeps saying it's just regular talk and I'm making it into something it's not. But I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful. It's definitely not for anyone else, doctor. It's directed right at me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear these messages when you are alone?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when nobody else is around. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. It sounds just like he's standing right there.\n\nDoctor: Do these messages and warnings from different sources seem to be related to each other in some way?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't really thought about it like that. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. But my old friend, when I hear him out on the porch, he ain't warning me about nothing. He just calls my name or hums a tune. So... no, I wouldn't say they have anything to do with each other. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. I didn't come in here to be talked out of it. Like I said, the men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they give those little messages meant just for me. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. I mean, we keep going in circles about this. I know what I hear. \n\nI came in here to tell you that those men playing dominoes, and that fellow on the radio... they are sending me messages. It's meant for me. My wife keeps saying it's just regular talk, but I know what a warning sounds like. They're telling me to be careful. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\nDoctor: Would anything be able to convince you that these messages are not real or not meant for you?\n\nPatient: I don't reckon so. I mean, Doctor, I came in here because the men at the domino table and sometimes the radio announcer give little messages meant for me. That's just the truth of it. Even when my wife says it is just talk, I know they are warning me to be careful. You can't really convince a man out of something he's hearing with his own ears, so no, I don't think anything could change my mind.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could potentially change your understanding of these messages in the future?\n\nPatient: I just don't see how. Like I keep telling you, I came in here because of the men at the domino table, and that radio announcer. They give these little messages that are meant for me. My wife keeps telling me it is just talk, but I know what I'm hearing. I know they are warning me to be careful. I don't see how the future is going to change what I'm hearing right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to express your thoughts clearly or stay on topic when talking to others?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any trouble with that. I just say what's on my mind. I don't get all mixed up or lose my place when I'm talking to folks, if that's what you mean. I just talk regular, same as I'm doing with you right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're not talking to someone, like when you're alone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't... I don't have that problem. When I'm sitting by myself at home, my thoughts stay right where they're supposed to. I just think about my day or, well, whatever needs doing around the house. They don't get all jumbled up or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself having trouble putting your thoughts into words when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. If I got something to explain, I just... well, I just tell 'em what I mean. The words come out fine. I don't really get stuck trying to find the right way to say things, if that's what you're asking. Like I said before, I just talk plain and regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period in your life where you heard voices or received messages like this before the past nine months?\n\nPatient: No, not before that. It's just been these past nine months or so. They kind of come and go, you know? The voices and those messages... they just started up about nine months ago, and it's been off and on since then. But nothing like that before.\n\nDoctor: Do you think anyone else hears these messages or is it only you?\n\nPatient: Well, my wife hears the words, but she don't hear the actual messages. She says it's just regular talk. But I know they're meant for me. Like the men down at the domino table, or sometimes the announcer on the radio... they give these little messages. Even if my wife thinks it's nothing, I know they're warning me to be careful. So I suppose I'm the only one who really understands what they're doing.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well, doctor, I came in because... well, I've been noticing some things lately. When I'm down playing dominoes with the men, they say things. Little messages, just meant for me. And it's not just them, sometimes the announcer on the radio does it too. My wife, she tells me it's just talk, that I'm making it up. But I know what I hear. I know they're warning me... telling me I need to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are meant for you specifically, or could they be for someone else?\n\nPatient: No, they're for me. I'm sure of it. Like I was saying, the men down at the domino table... they give these little messages, and it's meant just for me. Even the radio announcer does it sometimes. \n\nMy wife, she keeps saying it's just regular talk and I'm making it into something it's not. But I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful. It's definitely not for anyone else, doctor. It's directed right at me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear these messages when you are alone?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when nobody else is around. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. It sounds just like he's standing right there.\n\nDoctor: Do these messages and warnings from different sources seem to be related to each other in some way?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't really thought about it like that. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. But my old friend, when I hear him out on the porch, he ain't warning me about nothing. He just calls my name or hums a tune. So... no, I wouldn't say they have anything to do with each other. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. I didn't come in here to be talked out of it. Like I said, the men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they give those little messages meant just for me. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. I mean, we keep going in circles about this. I know what I hear. \n\nI came in here to tell you that those men playing dominoes, and that fellow on the radio... they are sending me messages. It's meant for me. My wife keeps saying it's just regular talk, but I know what a warning sounds like. They're telling me to be careful. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\nDoctor: Would anything be able to convince you that these messages are not real or not meant for you?\n\nPatient: I don't reckon so. I mean, Doctor, I came in here because the men at the domino table and sometimes the radio announcer give little messages meant for me. That's just the truth of it. Even when my wife says it is just talk, I know they are warning me to be careful. You can't really convince a man out of something he's hearing with his own ears, so no, I don't think anything could change my mind.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could potentially change your understanding of these messages in the future?\n\nPatient: I just don't see how. Like I keep telling you, I came in here because of the men at the domino table, and that radio announcer. They give these little messages that are meant for me. My wife keeps telling me it is just talk, but I know what I'm hearing. I know they are warning me to be careful. I don't see how the future is going to change what I'm hearing right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to express your thoughts clearly or stay on topic when talking to others?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any trouble with that. I just say what's on my mind. I don't get all mixed up or lose my place when I'm talking to folks, if that's what you mean. I just talk regular, same as I'm doing with you right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're not talking to someone, like when you're alone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't... I don't have that problem. When I'm sitting by myself at home, my thoughts stay right where they're supposed to. I just think about my day or, well, whatever needs doing around the house. They don't get all jumbled up or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself having trouble putting your thoughts into words when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. Even if my wife thinks it's nothing, I know they're warning me to be careful. So I suppose I'm the only one who really understands what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could make you doubt the messages you're hearing are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's directed right at me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear these messages when you are alone?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when nobody else is around. Like... well, some afternoons, when the house gets real quiet, I hear my old friend. I'll hear him call my name from out on the porch, or maybe just hum an old choir song. It's so clear, doctor. It sounds just like he's standing right there.\n\nDoctor: Do these messages and warnings from different sources seem to be related to each other in some way?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't really thought about it like that. I... I don't think they're connected, no. \n\nThe fellas at the table and the man on the radio, they're the ones giving me the warnings. But my old friend, when I hear him out on the porch, he ain't warning me about nothing. He just calls my name or hums a tune. So... no, I wouldn't say they have anything to do with each other. They're just different things I hear.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these messages and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, let me think on it... I'd say it's been about nine months now. Yeah, right around nine months. It's not every single day, you know, it just happens on and off. But that's about how long the voices and those special messages have been going on.\n\nDoctor: Have these messages and voices been causing you any problems in your daily life or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, they have. It's... it's gotten in the way of things I used to enjoy. I've missed a fair number of choir rehearsals lately because I just can't focus with all the noise. And my garden... well, I've let parts of it go. Just haven't had the energy or the mind to tend to it. \n\nAnd those domino games with the fellas... I don't join in like I used to. The warnings make me so tense, and I think it makes everyone else uncomfortable. So I just stay away.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's a larger plan or meaning behind all these messages and warnings you're receiving?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't know about any big, larger plan. I just know what I'm hearing. \n\nI came to see you because the men down at the domino table, and sometimes that announcer on the radio... they give these little messages that are meant just for me. My wife, she tells me it's just regular talk and I shouldn't pay it no mind. But... well, I know better. I know they are warning me to be careful. I might not know the whole plan, but I know a warning when I hear one.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that maybe your wife is right and you're misinterpreting these messages?\n\nPatient: Well, no, I don't. Like I was just saying a minute ago... I know she thinks it's just regular talk. She tells me that all the time. But I'm not misinterpreting it. \n\nThose men at the domino table, and that announcer on the radio... they are giving little messages that are meant just for me. Even when my wife says it's just talk and tries to wave it off, I know what I hear. I know they are warning me to be careful, plain as day. I can't just ignore it like she wants me to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that these messages might not be real, or has your mind been made up about their meaning from the start?\n\nPatient: Well, I know you and my wife both think I might be making this up or getting confused, but my mind is made up because I hear it. I didn't come in here to be talked out of it. Like I said, the men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they give those little messages meant just for me. My wife can say it's just talk all she wants, but I know a warning when I hear one. They are telling me to be careful, and I can't just pretend it's not happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to be wrong about these messages and warnings?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I'm wrong. I mean, we keep going in circles about this. I know what I hear. \n\nI came in here to tell you that those men playing dominoes, and that fellow on the radio... they are sending me messages. It's meant for me. My wife keeps saying it's just regular talk, but I know what a warning sounds like. They're telling me to be careful. I can't just be wrong about something I'm hearing with my own ears.\n\nDoctor: Would anything be able to convince you that these messages are not real or not meant for you?\n\nPatient: I don't reckon so. I mean, Doctor, I came in here because the men at the domino table and sometimes the radio announcer give little messages meant for me. That's just the truth of it. Even when my wife says it is just talk, I know they are warning me to be careful. You can't really convince a man out of something he's hearing with his own ears, so no, I don't think anything could change my mind.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could potentially change your understanding of these messages in the future?\n\nPatient: I just don't see how. Like I keep telling you, I came in here because of the men at the domino table, and that radio announcer. They give these little messages that are meant for me. My wife keeps telling me it is just talk, but I know what I'm hearing. I know they are warning me to be careful. I don't see how the future is going to change what I'm hearing right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to express your thoughts clearly or stay on topic when talking to others?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any trouble with that. I just say what's on my mind. I don't get all mixed up or lose my place when I'm talking to folks, if that's what you mean. I just talk regular, same as I'm doing with you right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're not talking to someone, like when you're alone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't... I don't have that problem. When I'm sitting by myself at home, my thoughts stay right where they're supposed to. I just think about my day or, well, whatever needs doing around the house. They don't get all jumbled up or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself having trouble putting your thoughts into words when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. If I got something to explain, I just... well, I just tell 'em what I mean. The words come out fine. I don't really get stuck trying to find the right way to say things, if that's what you're asking. Like I said before, I just talk plain and regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period in your life where you heard voices or received messages like this before the past nine months?\n\nPatient: No, not before that. It's just been these past nine months or so. They kind of come and go, you know? The voices and those messages... they just started up about nine months ago, and it's been off and on since then. But nothing like that before.\n\nDoctor: Do you think anyone else hears these messages or is it only you?\n\nPatient: Well, my wife hears the words, but she don't hear the actual messages. She says it's just regular talk. But I know they're meant for me. Like the men down at the domino table, or sometimes the announcer on the radio... they give these little messages. Even if my wife thinks it's nothing, I know they're warning me to be careful. So I suppose I'm the only one who really understands what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Is there anything that could make you doubt the messages you're hearing are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I know my wife says it's just regular talk, but... well, I came here because those men at the domino table and that radio announcer, they are giving me little messages. I hear them. They're warning me to be careful. So, no matter what my wife thinks, I know what I'm hearing is real.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another when talking to others without finishing what you started to say?\n\nPatient: Well, sometimes, yeah. My mind just gets to running so fast. I'll be thinking about the church choir, and then suddenly I'm on about baseball, and then I'm thinking about some repairs that need doing around the house, and then my friend... and it just goes back and forth. It moves so quick that I can't even hold onto one thought long enough to explain it right to anybody. I just jump right to the next thing."
